\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{=}}\bar{\alpha}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}^{3/2}}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\bigg]+O((1-\alpha_{t})^{2})

\displaystyle=\bar{\alpha}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{:y}[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}^{3/2}}[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}^{2}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}[\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}\right)\begin{pmatrix}0&I_{d-p}\end{pmatrix}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+O((1-\alpha_{t})^{2})

\displaystyle=\bar{\alpha}_{t}\mathrm{Tr}\bigg([\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{:y}[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}^{3/2}}[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}^{2}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}[\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\begin{pmatrix}0&I_{d-p}\end{pmatrix}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O((1-\alpha_{t})^{2})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\leq}}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{:y}\right\rVert\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\times

\displaystyle\qquad\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}^{3/2}}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}\right\rVert^{2}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}\right\rVert\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}\right\rVert\right)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\mathrm{Tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iv)}}{{\lesssim}}\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}^{3/2}}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\right)\max\left\{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2}),d\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\alpha_{t})\left(\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d\right)

where
(ii)
follows by
Lemma

9
and from definition that
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0|y}=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0}
,
(iii)
follows because
\left|\mathrm{Tr}(UV)\right|\leq\left\lVert U\right\rVert\mathrm{Tr}(V)
if
V
is positive semi-definite, and
(iv)
follows from (
58
) and the same reasons for (
57
). In particular, we note that
[\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}=[\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}=(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}
and
\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}}<\infty
.

For the second term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
, we use the fact that
[\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}=(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-1}
and have

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{\intercal}\nabla^{2}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})

\displaystyle=\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\mathrm{Tr}\bigg([\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{:y}[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}\bigg)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(v)}}{{\leq}}\max\left\{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2}),d\right\}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim\max\left\{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2}),d\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d.

Here
(v)
follows from the fact that
\left|\mathrm{Tr}(UV)\right|\leq\left\lVert U\right\rVert\mathrm{Tr}(V)
if
V
is positive semi-definite and (
58
).
The proof is complete by plugging all the results above into
Theorem

1
.

Remark 1
.

Before we end the proof, we leave a note for the case of
\sigma_{y}^{2}=0
(indeed, for any general
\sigma_{y}^{2}\geq 0
). The only difference is how to upper-bound
\mathcal{W}_{\text{oracle}}
. In particular, if
\sigma_{y}^{2}=0
, (
37
) no longer holds (i.e., we can no longer upper-bound
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\right\rVert
as a constant). Instead, we can obtain an upper bound as
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\right\rVert\lesssim(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})^{-1}
. Then, with the
\alpha_{t}
in (
8
), we have

\mathcal{W}_{\text{oracle}}\lesssim\frac{d(\log T)^{2}\log(1/\delta)^{2}}{T}+(\log T)\varepsilon^{2}.

The rest of the proof still follows because the
\alpha_{t}
satisfies
Definition

1
when
t\geq 2
.
Combining with
Lemma

7
, we would finally obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(Q_{1|y}\|\widehat{P}_{1|y})\lesssim(\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d)\left(1-\frac{3\log(1/\delta)\log T}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+(\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d)\frac{(\log T)^{2}\log(1/\delta)^{2}}{T}+\sqrt{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d}\cdot(\log T)\varepsilon.

Here
\mathrm{W}_{2}(Q_{1|y},Q_{0|y})^{2}\lesssim\delta d
.

I.4
Proof of
Proposition

1

We first introduce some useful notations for this subsection.
Recall that
Q_{0}
has mixture p.d.f. in which the mixture prior
\pi_{n}
is independent of
y\ (=Hx_{0}+n)
. Thus, using the fact that
x_{0}=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)x_{0}+H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}n
, we can define
Q_{0,n|y}
as (cf.
[
62
]
)

\displaystyle Q_{0|y}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}Q_{0,n|y}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\mathcal{N}((I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0,n}+H^{\dagger}y,(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}).

Note that when
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
and
\sigma_{y}^{2}>0
,
q_{0|y}
exists.
From the conditional forward model in (
5
), we further define

\displaystyle Q_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}Q_{t,n},\penalty\ \penalty\ Q_{t|y}=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}Q_{t,n|y},\penalty\ \penalty\ Q_{t,n}:=\mathcal{N}(\mu_{t,n},\Sigma_{t}),\penalty\ \penalty\ Q_{t,n|y}:=\mathcal{N}(\mu_{t,n|y},\Sigma_{t|y})

\displaystyle\mu_{t,n}

\displaystyle:=\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n},\penalty\ \penalty\ \Sigma_{t}:=\bar{\alpha}_{t}\Sigma_{0}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d},\penalty\ \penalty\ \mu_{t,n|y}:=\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0,n}+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t|y}

\displaystyle:=\Sigma_{t,sig}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal},\penalty\ \penalty\ \Sigma_{t,sig}:=\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d}.

(38)

Similar to (
37
), we still have

\left\lVert\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\right\rVert\lesssim 1,\quad\forall t\geq 1.

We can also calculate the scores of
Q_{t}
and
Q_{t|y}
in as follows.

\displaystyle\nabla\log q_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n}),

\displaystyle\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y}).

(39)

Now, with
f_{t,y}=f_{t,y}^{*}
(in (
10
)), from the expression of
\Delta_{t,y}
in (
32
), under the assumption
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
, the score mismatch at each diffusion step is equal to

\displaystyle\Delta_{t,y}

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(\nabla\log q_{t|y}-\nabla\log q_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n}(x_{t})(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n|y})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(\frac{q_{t,n}(x_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\right)(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n|y})\right)

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(\frac{q_{t,n}(x_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\right)(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\mu_{0,n}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n|y})\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(\frac{q_{t,n}(x_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\right)(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\mu_{0,n}

\displaystyle\quad-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}A_{t}\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})

(40)

where
A_{t}:=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)
. Here
(i)
follows from similar arguments as in (
56
). Note that since
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
, we have equivalently
A_{t}=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)
.
Since
H^{\dagger}H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}
, we can also re-express the second term in
\Delta_{t,y}
such that
[\Delta_{t,y}]_{y}=0
and

\displaystyle[\Delta_{t,y}]_{\bar{y}}

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(\frac{q_{t,n}(x_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\right)[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}

\displaystyle\quad-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})

(41)

since when
H^{\dagger}H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}
,
A_{t}=\begin{pmatrix}0&0\\
0&(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\end{pmatrix}
.

Now, for the second moment, we follow similar analyses in (
57
) and get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\bar{\alpha}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}\right\rVert^{2}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\mathbb{E}_{N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|y}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,N|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}_{N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|y}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

where
(ii)
follows from (
58
) and note that
Q_{t,N|y}
is Gaussian for each
N=n
.
Denote
\lambda_{1}\geq\dots\geq\lambda_{d}>0
and
\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\geq\dots\geq\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p}>0
to be the eigenvalues of
\Sigma_{0}
and
[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}
, respectively. Similarly as the proof of
Proposition

2
, we have
\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Sigma_{0}\right\rVert=\lambda_{1}
,
\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right\rVert\leq(\bar{\alpha}_{t}\lambda_{d}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t}))^{-1}\leq\frac{1}{\min\{\lambda_{d},1\}}
,
\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\bar{\alpha}_{t}\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})}\leq\frac{1}{\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}}
, and
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}\right\rVert\leq\bar{\alpha}_{t}(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2})+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\alpha}_{t}d+\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\frac{\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}\right\rVert^{2}}{\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}^{2}\min\{\lambda_{d},1\}^{2}}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\left(d(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\alpha}_{t}d+\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\frac{\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}\right\rVert^{2}}{\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}^{2}\min\{\lambda_{d},1\}^{2}}\max\left\{d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2})+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2},d\right\}.

The proof is complete.

I.5
Proof of
Theorem

5

We first recall all the notations in (
38
) under Gaussian mixture.
We also recall the scores from (
39
):

\displaystyle\nabla\log q_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n})

\displaystyle\qquad=-\Sigma_{t}^{-1}x_{t}+\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\mu_{t,n}

\displaystyle\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y})

\displaystyle\qquad=-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}x_{t}+\frac{1}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\mu_{t,n|y}

Also, we recall the explicit expression of
\Delta_{t,y}
from (
41
), such that
[\Delta_{t,y}]_{y}=0
and

\displaystyle[\Delta_{t,y}]_{\bar{y}}

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(\frac{q_{t,n}(x_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\right)[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}

\displaystyle\quad-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}).

In order to invoke
Theorem

1
, we need to check
3
and
4
. From
[
25
, Lemmas 13 and 14]
, since
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right\rVert\lesssim 1
for all
t\geq 0
, the absolute values of any-order partial derivative are bounded by
O(1)
in expectation, and thus
Assumption

3
is satisfied.
The following lemma verifies
Assumption

4
using the
\alpha_{t}
in
Definition

1
.

Lemma 8
.

Under the same condition of
Theorem

5
,
Assumption

4
holds if the
\alpha_{t}
satisfies
Definition

1
.

Proof.

See
Section

J.2
.
∎

Now we start to upper-bound the conditional KL-divergence of interest.
Recall that from
Theorem

1
,
\mathrm{KL}(Q_{0|y}\|\widehat{P}_{0|y})\lesssim\mathcal{W}_{\text{oracle}}+\mathcal{W}_{\text{bias}}+\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
,
where

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{\text{oracle}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{2\alpha_{t}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\mathrm{Tr}\Big(\nabla^{2}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\nabla^{2}\log q_{t|y}(X_{t})\Big)\right]+(\log T)\varepsilon^{2}

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{\text{bias}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}(1-\alpha_{t})\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(\nabla\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t}))^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\bigg]

\displaystyle-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{2\alpha_{t}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{\intercal}\nabla^{2}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{3!{\alpha_{t}}^{3/2}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[3\sum_{i=1}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iii}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j=1\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iij}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\max_{t\geq 1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}}(\log T)\varepsilon.

From
[
25
, Theorem 2]
(and by assumption
N\leq d
), if the
\alpha_{t}
satisfies
Definition

1
,

\mathcal{W}_{\text{oracle}}\lesssim\frac{d^{2}(\log T)^{2}}{T}+(\log T)\varepsilon^{2}.

Also, from
Proposition

1
, under the assumption on
\alpha_{t}
,

\mathcal{W}_{\text{bias}}
can be upper-bounded as

\mathcal{W}_{\text{bias}}\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}.

Among the terms in
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
, the last estimation error term can be upper-bounded using
Proposition

1
as

\max_{t\geq 1}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}}(\log T)\varepsilon\lesssim\sqrt{d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}(\log T)\varepsilon.

It remains to analyze the rest of the terms in
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
.
In the following we fix
t\geq 1
.
We remind readers of the notations in (
38
).
For the first term in
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
, we first provide the following useful calculations. Note that by exchanging the order of expectation, for any function
\mathrm{fn}
we have

\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\mathrm{fn}(X_{t},n)\right]=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\mathrm{fn}(X_{t},n).

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\left|(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})^{\intercal}X_{t}\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left|(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})^{\intercal}X_{t}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

(42)

where
(i)
follows from (
58
).

Also, note that
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}(H^{\dagger}H)=0
using the following simple induction argument. For the base case, we have
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)=0
from
Lemma

9
. Then, suppose
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-(r-1)}(H^{\dagger}H)=0
, we have
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}(H^{\dagger}H)=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-(r-1)}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H+H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-(r-1)}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)+(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-(r-1)}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)=0
.
Thus, for all
r\geq 1
and any fixed vector
v
, with the definition of
\Delta_{t,y}
in (
40
) and (
41
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}v)^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert v\right\rVert\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert=\left\lVert v\right\rVert\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert A_{t-1}^{r}\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\lVert v\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\lVert v\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert\times

\displaystyle\qquad\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

(43)

where the last line follows from (
58
). Similarly,

\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}v)^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}.

Also, for all
r\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}X_{t})^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|X_{t}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|X_{t}^{\intercal}A_{t-1}^{r}\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert\times

\displaystyle\qquad\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\left|(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})^{\intercal}X_{t}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

(44)

where the last line follows from (
42
) and the fact that, from (
58
),

\displaystyle\sqrt{d}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{d}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\sqrt{2\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{d}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}+d}

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sqrt{d}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}.

Similarly, we also have
\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}X_{t})^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}
.

Also, for all
r\geq 1
, using the expression of
\nabla\log q_{t|y}
in (
39
), and noting that by definition
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y})=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-r}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t}))^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\left(A_{t-1}^{r}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}A_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(X_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y})\right)^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\times

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\sum_{\ell=1}^{N}\pi_{\ell}\frac{q_{t,\ell|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}[X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,\ell}]_{\bar{y}}\right\rVert\times\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\times

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N,L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\bigg[[X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,L}]_{\bar{y}}^{\intercal}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N})\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}\times\sqrt{d}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N,L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,L}\right\rVert\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

(45)

where the last line follows because, from (
58
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N,L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left[\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,L}\right\rVert\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,L}\right\rVert^{2}}\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}.

Now, we start to analyze the first term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
. Recall that
m_{t,y}(x_{t})=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t-1}\sim Q_{t-1|t,y}}[X_{t-1}]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})
. Using the score expressions in (
39
), we can calculate that given
x_{t}
(and thus
m_{t,y}=m_{t,y}(x_{t})
),

\displaystyle\nabla\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}x_{t}-\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}m_{t,y}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})}{q_{t|y}(x_{t})}\mu_{t,n|y}+\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\mu_{t-1,n|y}

\displaystyle=\left(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\right)\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}x_{t}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(x_{t}+\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t}))

\displaystyle\qquad+(1-\alpha_{t})\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\mu_{t-1,n|y}

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha_{t}\frac{\sum_{n,\ell=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\pi_{\ell}\left(q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,\ell|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-q_{t-1,\ell|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right)\mu_{t-1,n|y}}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t|y}(x_{t})}.

Here, using similar analyses as in the proof of
Lemma

5
, we get

\displaystyle(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,n|y})-(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y})=\frac{1-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})-(1-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}})\mu_{t-1,n|y}

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}=\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\Sigma_{t-1,n}^{-1}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}}\Sigma_{t-1,n}^{-2}+O((1-\alpha_{t})^{2})

\displaystyle q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,\ell|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-q_{t-1,\ell|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,n|y}(x_{t})\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle=q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,\ell|y}(x_{t})(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad+(q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,\ell|y}(x_{t})-q_{t-1,\ell|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,n|y}(x_{t}))\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle=q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,\ell|y}(x_{t})(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\frac{1}{2}((m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,\ell|y})-(x_{t}-\mu_{t,\ell|y}))^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,\ell|y})

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,\ell|y})^{\intercal}(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1})(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,\ell|y})

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,\ell|y})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}((m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,\ell|y})-(x_{t}-\mu_{t,\ell|y}))

\displaystyle\quad\qquad-\frac{1}{2}((m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,n|y})-(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y}))^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,n|y})

\displaystyle\quad\qquad-\frac{1}{2}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y})^{\intercal}(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1})(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,n|y})

\displaystyle\quad\qquad-\frac{1}{2}(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}((m_{t,y}-\mu_{t-1,n|y})-(x_{t}-\mu_{t,n|y}))\bigg)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+O((1-\alpha_{t})^{2})

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(\nabla\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\nabla\log q_{t|y})^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\bigg]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[\left|X_{t}^{\intercal}(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}-\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1})\Delta_{t,y}\right|\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[\left|(X_{t}+\nabla\log q_{t|y})^{\intercal}(\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1})\Delta_{t,y}\right|\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\alpha_{t})\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[\left|\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\mu_{t-1,n|y}\right)^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\sum_{n,\ell=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\pi_{\ell}\left(\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,\ell|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}-\frac{q_{t-1,\ell|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right)\mu_{t-1,n|y}\right|.

Among the four terms above, the first term
\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}
from (
44
) (along with the similar result for
\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}
), the second term
\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}
from (
44
) and (
45
), and both the third and the fourth term
\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}
from (
43
) (along with the similar result for
\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}
). Thus,

\left|\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(\nabla\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t}))^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\bigg]\right|\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2},

and the first term in
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
satisfies that

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[(\nabla\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t}))^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\bigg]\\
\lesssim\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)\frac{\log(1/\delta)^{2}(\log T)^{2}}{T}.

For the second term in
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
, we first provide the following useful calculation.
Similar to (
43
), for all
r\geq 1
and any fixed vector
v
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}v)^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert v\right\rVert^{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}=\left\lVert v\right\rVert^{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert A_{t}^{r}\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\lVert v\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert^{2}\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert v\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert^{2}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}+d\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

(46)

where the last line follows from (
58
).

Also, similar to (
44
), for all
r\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}m_{t,y})^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|X_{t}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}+(1-\alpha_{t})\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t}))^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\lesssim}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|X_{t}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|X_{t}^{\intercal}A_{t}^{r}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert^{2}\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-r}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert^{2}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\left|(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})^{\intercal}X_{t}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}

(47)

where
(ii)
follows from the fact that
\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t}))^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-r}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}\lesssim d^{2}
(using a similar argument for deriving (
45
)), and the last line follows because

\displaystyle d\cdot\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq d\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(2\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\lesssim}}d\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left(\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}+d\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}+d\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}

where
(iii)
follows from (
58
), and also

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t,n|y}(X_{t})}{q_{t|y}(X_{t})}\left|(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n})^{\intercal}X_{t}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left((X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N})^{\intercal}X_{t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{4}+2\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{4}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{N\sim\Pi}\left\lVert\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}

where the last line above follows because for all
r\geq 1
and each
n\in[N]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n|y}\right\rVert^{r}+\left\lVert\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n|y}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert^{r}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t,n|y}}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,n|y})\right\rVert^{r}+(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{r/2}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{r/2}+\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{r}.

(48)

Now we are ready to analyze the second term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
. Note that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\left(\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right)-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})\right)\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})\right)^{\intercal}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\nabla^{2}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 3\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\left(\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+3\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+3\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+6\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left[\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\left(\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})\right)^{2}\right].

To determine the rate of these two terms, we get

\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}^{\intercal}A_{t}\Delta_{t,y}\right|\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\frac{q_{t-1,n|y}(m_{t,y})}{q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y})}\left|(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\max_{n\in[N]}\left|(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|m_{t,y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\max_{n\in[N]}\left|\mu_{t,n|y}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\Delta_{t,y}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim N\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)

where the last line follows from (
46
) and (
47
).
Thus, the second term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
satisfies that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{2\alpha_{t}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{\intercal}\nabla^{2}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim N\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)\frac{c^{2}(\log T)^{2}}{T}.

For the third term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
, we provide the following useful calculations. Denote
v^{\circ 3}
as the element-wise (Hadamard) third power of a vector
v
. For each
n\in[N]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})^{\circ 3}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Delta_{t,y}\right|=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|[\Delta_{t,y}]_{\bar{y}}^{\intercal}[m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n}]^{\circ 3}_{\bar{y}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|[\Delta_{t,y}]_{\bar{y}}^{\intercal}[X_{t}-\mu_{t,n}]^{\circ 3}_{\bar{y}}\right|+(1-\alpha_{t})\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t})\right\rVert_{6}^{6}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iv)}}{{\lesssim}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|[\Delta_{t,y}]_{\bar{y}}^{\intercal}[X_{t}-\mu_{t,n}]^{\circ 3}_{\bar{y}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{\ell}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}:}\mu_{0,\ell}\right\rVert\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert_{6}^{6}}+\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,L}\right\rVert_{4}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,L}\right\rVert^{6}}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{L\sim\Pi}\left\lVert\mu_{t,n}-\mu_{t,L}\right\rVert^{6}}+\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,L}\right\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}

(49)

where
(iv)
follows from
Lemma

6
(using the
\alpha_{t}
in (
8
)) and
[
25
, Lemma 15]
, and the last line follows from (
48
). With a similar argument,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y})(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Delta_{t,y}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,L}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}.

(50)

Now, employing the notations from
[
25
, Section G.1]
, we define

z_{t,n}(x):=\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x-\mu_{t,n|y}),\quad\xi_{t}(x,i):=\max_{n}\left|z_{t,n}^{i}(x)\right|,\quad\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ij}:=\max_{n}\left|[\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}]^{ij}\right|.

When
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
, we note that

\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}=\begin{pmatrix}(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2})^{-1}I_{p}&0\\
0&(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\end{pmatrix}.

Thus, we have
\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ij}\equiv 0
whenever
(i,j)\in[1,p]\times[p+1,d]
or
(i,j)\in[p+1,d]\times[1,p]
and
\max_{i,j\in[p+1,d]}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ij}=O(1)
.
Since
\sigma_{y}^{2}>0
, we also have
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right\rVert\lesssim 1
.

From
[
25
, Section G.1.2]
, an upper bound for third-order partial derivatives is

\left|\partial^{3}_{ijk}\log q_{t|y}(x)\right|\leq 6\xi_{t}(x,i)\xi_{t}(x,j)\xi_{t}(x,k)+2\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ij}\xi_{t}(x,k)+2\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ik}\xi_{t}(x,j)+2\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{jk}\xi_{t}(x,i).

We also remind readers that
\Delta_{t,y}
is supported on
\mathrm{range}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)
, namely that
[\Delta_{t,y}]_{y}\equiv 0
.

Now, the third term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
can be upper-bounded as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iii}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iii}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}),i)^{3}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}\right|+\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ii}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}),i)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{n=1}^{N}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert^{3}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}(m_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}-\mu_{t,n|y}^{i})^{3}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}(m_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}-\mu_{t,n|y}^{i})\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim N\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right).

Here the last line follows from (
49
) and (
50
).

We provide the following useful calculations to upper-bound the fourth term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
. First, for all
r\geq 1
and any fixed vector
v
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert m_{t,y}-v\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}X_{t}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t})-v\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-v\right\rVert^{r}+(1-\alpha_{t})\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\nabla\log q_{t|y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(v)}}{{\lesssim}}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
L\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\mu_{t,L}\right\rVert^{r}+\mathbb{E}_{L\sim\Pi}\left\lVert\mu_{t,L}-v\right\rVert^{r}

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{r/2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{r}

(51)

where
(v)
follows from
Lemma

6
(using the
\alpha_{t}
in (
8
)) and
[
25
, Lemma 15]
, and the last line follows from (
48
).
Now,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},i)^{2}\left|\sum_{j=p+1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},j)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{n,\ell=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y}\right\rVert^{2}\left|(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,\ell|y})^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n,\ell=1}^{N}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n|y}\right\rVert^{4}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,\ell|y}\right\rVert^{4}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{4}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{2}\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)

(52)

where the first inequality follows from
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right\rVert\lesssim 1
and the last line follows from (
51
) and (
59
).
Also,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i,j=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ii}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},j)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ii}\right|\cdot\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{j=p+1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},j)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ii}\right|\cdot\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|(m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n})^{\intercal}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ii}\right|\cdot\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert^{2}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim Nd\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

(53)

where the last line follows from
Proposition

1
and (
51
).
Also,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i,j=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ij}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},i)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left(\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},i)\right)\left|\sum_{j=p+1}^{d}\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{d\cdot\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left(\sum_{i=p+1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y},i)^{2}\right)}\sqrt{d\cdot\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{d\cdot\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert m_{t,y}-\mu_{t,n}\right\rVert^{2}}\sqrt{d\cdot\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{N}d\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

(54)

where the last line follows from
Proposition

1
and (
51
).

Now, the fourth term of
\mathcal{W}_{\text{vanish}}
can be upper-bounded as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i,j=1}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iij}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{j=p+1}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iij}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}),i)^{2}\left|\sum_{j=p+1}^{d}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}),j)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i,j=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ii}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}),j)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left|\sum_{i,j=p+1}^{d}\bar{\Sigma}_{t}^{ij}\xi_{t}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}),i)\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\right|.

For the three terms above, the first term
\lesssim N^{2}\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)
from (
52
), the second term
\lesssim Nd\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)
from (
53
), and the last term
\lesssim\sqrt{N}d\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)
from (
54
).

Thus, overall, with the
\alpha_{t}
in (
8
) (cf.
Lemma

6
), the third and fourth terms give us

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{3!{\alpha_{t}}^{3/2}}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\bigg[3\sum_{i=1}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iii}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{i}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j=1\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}^{d}\partial^{3}_{iij}\log q_{t-1|y}(m_{t,y}(X_{t}))\Delta_{t,y}(X_{t})^{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{2}\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)\frac{(\log T)^{2}}{T}.

Therefore, combining all the above, since
N
is constant,

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(Q_{0|y}\|\widehat{P}_{0|y})

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)\frac{(\log T)^{2}}{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}(\log T)\varepsilon.

Remark 2
.

In case of general
\sigma_{y}\geq 0
, the same upper bound can be applied to
\left\lVert\Sigma_{t-1|y}^{-1}\right\rVert
as detailed in
Remark

1
. Thus, with the
\alpha_{t}
in (
8
), we similarly have

\mathcal{W}_{\text{oracle}}\lesssim\frac{d^{2}(\log T)^{2}\log(1/\delta)^{2}}{T}+(\log T)\varepsilon^{2}.

The rest of the proof is similar.
Combining with
Lemma

7
, we would finally obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(Q_{1|y}\|\widehat{P}_{1|y})\lesssim\left(d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}\right)\left(1-\frac{2\log(1/\delta)\log T}{T}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(d^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{4}\right)\frac{(\log T)^{2}\log(1/\delta)^{2}}{T}+\sqrt{d+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{2}}(\log T)\varepsilon.

Here
\mathrm{W}_{2}(Q_{1|y},Q_{0|y})^{2}\lesssim\delta d
.

Appendix J
Auxiliary Lemmas and Proofs in
Section

4

J.1
Proof of
Proposition

2

Given
Q_{0}=\mathcal{N}(\mu_{0},\Sigma_{0})
, from the conditional forward model in (
5
), we can calculate

\displaystyle Q_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{N}(\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0},\bar{\alpha}_{t}\Sigma_{0}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d})=:\mathcal{N}(\mu_{t},\Sigma_{t})

\displaystyle Q_{t|y}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{N}(\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0}+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y,

\displaystyle\qquad\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d}).

Note that when
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
and
\sigma_{y}^{2}>0
,
q_{0|y}
exists. Define

\displaystyle\mu_{t|y}

\displaystyle:=\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0}+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t,sig}

\displaystyle:=\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d}

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t|y}

\displaystyle:=\Sigma_{t,sig}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}.

(55)

Here
\Sigma_{t,sig}
is the signal variance at time
t
, and
\Sigma_{t|y}
is the total variance of the signal and the measurement noise. Note that when
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
,
[\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}=[\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}
.
We also calculate the respective scores of
Q_{t}
and
Q_{t|y}
:

\nabla\log q_{t}(x_{t})=-\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\mu_{t}),\quad\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})=-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\mu_{t|y}).

Since
f_{t,y}=f_{t,y}^{*}
(defined in (
10
)), from (
32
), the bias at each time is equal to

\displaystyle\Delta_{t,y}

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(\nabla\log q_{t|y}(x_{t})-\nabla\log q_{t}(x_{t}))

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|y})\right)

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\quad-(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y).

Now, define

\displaystyle V_{t}

\displaystyle:=(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)+(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle A_{t}

\displaystyle:=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)

Thus, we have
\Sigma_{t}=\Sigma_{t,sig}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}V_{t}
and
\Sigma_{t|y}=\Sigma_{t,sig}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}
. By Woodbury matrix identity, for any two matrices
A
and
B
, their sum can be inversed as
(A+B)^{-1}=A^{-1}-A^{-1}B(A+B)^{-1}
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t}^{-1}

\displaystyle=(\Sigma_{t,sig}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}V_{t})^{-1}=\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}V_{t}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle=(\Sigma_{t,sig}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal})^{-1}=\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}

where
(i)
holds under assumption
H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\end{pmatrix}
.
Thus,

\displaystyle\Delta_{t,y}

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})-\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|y})\right)

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}V_{t}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\quad-(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t|y}^{-1}\right)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{=}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\left(\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}V_{t}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\quad-(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\mu_{0}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y)

\displaystyle=-\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}V_{t}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}\Big((I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})+H^{\dagger}H(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}\Big((I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})+((H^{\dagger}H)x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}H^{\dagger}y)\Big)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{=}}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}V_{t}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iv)}}{{=}}-\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

\displaystyle=-\bar{\alpha}_{t}A_{t}\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})

(56)

where
(ii)
–
(iv)
hold because
(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)=0
by
Lemma

9
.

Now, since
H^{\dagger}H=\begin{pmatrix}I_{p}&0\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}
, we can re-express
A_{t}
and
\Delta_{t,y}
as follows.

\displaystyle A_{t}

\displaystyle=(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&0\\
0&I_{d-p}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{p}&0\\
0&\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p}\end{pmatrix}^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}0&0\\
0&I_{d-p}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&0\\
0&(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\Delta_{t,y}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
-\bar{\alpha}_{t}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\end{pmatrix},

and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}=\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\mathrm{Tr}\bigg((\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}

\displaystyle\qquad[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{:y}[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\bigg)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(v)}}{{\leq}}\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}\right\rVert\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}

(57)

where
(v)
follows from the fact that
\left|\mathrm{Tr}(UV)\right|\leq\left\lVert U\right\rVert\mathrm{Tr}(V)
if
V
is positive semi-definite.
To analyze each norm above, denote
\lambda_{1}\geq\dots\geq\lambda_{d}>0
to be the eigenvalues of
\Sigma_{0}
, and note that
\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Sigma_{0}\right\rVert=\lambda_{1}
. The largest eigenvalue of
\Sigma_{t}^{-1}
is
(\bar{\alpha}_{t}\lambda_{d}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t}))^{-1}
, and note that
\left\lVert[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right\rVert
.
Also, since
[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}
is positive semi-definite, denote
\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\geq\dots\geq\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p}>0
to be its eigenvalues, and thus

\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\bar{\alpha}_{t}\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})}\leq\frac{1}{\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}}<\infty.

Also, since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})^{\intercal}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}(X_{t}-\mu_{t|y}+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}))(X_{t}-\mu_{t|y}+\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}))^{\intercal}

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{t|y}+\bar{\alpha}_{t}(H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0})(H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0})^{\intercal},

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}=\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{t|y})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(vi)}}{{\leq}}\bar{\alpha}_{t}(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2})+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2}),d\right\}

(58)

where
(vi)
is because
\Sigma_{t|y}
is positive definite with

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bar{\alpha}_{t}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\alpha}_{t}\left\lVert\Sigma_{0}\right\rVert+\bar{\alpha}_{t}\sigma_{y}^{2}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})

\displaystyle=\bar{\alpha}_{t}(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2})+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t}).

Therefore, for the second moment,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\frac{\max\left\{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2}),d\right\}}{\min\{\lambda_{d},1\}^{2}\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}^{2}}\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\cdot\max\left\{\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{2}+d(\lambda_{1}+\sigma_{y}^{2}),d\right\}

since
\left\lVert[\Sigma_{0}]_{y\bar{y}}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert\Sigma_{0}\right\rVert^{2}=\lambda_{1}^{2}
.
Also, for general moments with
m\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{m}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0})\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{m}\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}\right\rVert^{m}\left\lVert\Sigma_{0}\right\rVert^{m}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right\rVert^{m}\left\lVert x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{m}\left\lVert x_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0}\right\rVert^{m}

and thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{m}\left(\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t|y}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(X_{t}-\mu_{t|y})\right\rVert^{m}+\left\lVert\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0})\right\rVert^{m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{m}\left((m-1)!!\cdot d^{m/2-1}+\left\lVert\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}(H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0})\right\rVert^{m}\right)=O(\bar{\alpha}_{t}).

Therefore,
Assumption

4
is satisfied if the
\alpha_{t}
satisfies
Definition

1
. The proof is now complete.

J.2
Proof of
Lemma

8

We continue from the expression of
\Delta_{t,y}
in (
40
) when
Q_{0}
is Gaussian mixture. For
m\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}}\left\lVert\Delta_{t,y}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{m-1}(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m/2}\max_{n\in[N]}\left\lVert\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{m-1}(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}(X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N})\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\lesssim}}(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m/2}d^{m/2}+(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}X_{t}\sim Q_{t|y}\\
N\sim\Pi_{\cdot|t,y}(\cdot|X_{t})\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle=(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m/2}d^{m/2}+(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}N\sim\Pi\\
X_{t}\sim Q_{t,N|y}\end{subarray}}\left\lVert X_{t}-\sqrt{\bar{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0,N}\right\rVert^{m}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\lesssim}}(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m/2}d^{m/2}+(\bar{\alpha}_{t})^{m}\left(d^{m/2}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\pi_{n}\left\lVert H^{\dagger}y-H^{\dagger}H\mu_{0,n}\right\rVert^{m}\right)

(59)

\displaystyle=O(\bar{\alpha}_{t}).

Here
(ii)
follows because

\displaystyle\left\lVert(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t,sig}^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{0}(H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert(\bar{\alpha}_{t}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}\bar{y}}+(1-\bar{\alpha}_{t})I_{d-p})^{-1}[\Sigma_{0}]_{\bar{y}y}[\Sigma_{t}^{-1}]_{y:}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{1}}{\min\{\tilde{\lambda}_{d-p},1\}\min\{\lambda_{d},1\}}=O(1),

and
(iii)
follows from (
48
).
Therefore, this verifies
Assumption

4
when the
\alpha_{t}
satisfies
Definition

1
. The proof is now complete.

J.3
Lemma

9
and its proof

Lemma 9
.

Given a positive semi-definite matrix
\Sigma
,
\sigma\geq 0
, and
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle(\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}+(1-\alpha)I_{d})^{-1}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)=\frac{1}{1-\alpha}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(\alpha(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)+\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}H+(1-\alpha)I_{d})^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)=0.

Proof.

The key of the proof is the Woodbury matrix identity, which states that for any matrices
U\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times p},V\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times d}
,

(I_{d}+UV)^{-1}=I_{d}-U(I_{p}+VU)^{-1}V.

For the first equality, we apply Woodbury with
U=\sqrt{\frac{\alpha\sigma}{1-\alpha}}H^{\dagger}
and
V=\sqrt{\frac{\alpha\sigma}{1-\alpha}}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}
and we get

(\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}+(1-\alpha)I_{d})^{-1}=\frac{1}{1-\alpha}\left(I_{d}-\frac{\alpha\sigma}{1-\alpha}H^{\dagger}\left(I_{p}+\frac{\alpha\sigma}{1-\alpha}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}H^{\dagger}\right)^{-1}(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}\right).

Since
p\leq d
, the pseudo-inverse equals
H^{\dagger}=H^{\intercal}(HH^{\intercal})^{-1}
, and by the orthogonal property we have

(H^{\dagger})^{\intercal}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)=(HH^{\intercal})^{-1}H(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)=0.

We have thus shown the first equality.

For the second equality, we first consider the case where
\sigma=0
. Write
S=(1-\alpha)I_{d}+\alpha(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)
. Since
\Sigma
is positive semi-definite, there exists matrix
L
such that
\Sigma=LL^{\intercal}
. Thus,

\displaystyle S^{-1}

\displaystyle=\bigg((1-\alpha)I_{d}+\alpha(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\Sigma(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)\bigg)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\bigg((1-\alpha)I_{d}+\alpha\Big((I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)L\Big)\Big((I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)^{\intercal}L\Big)^{\intercal}\bigg)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\alpha}I_{d}-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}((I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)L)\left(I_{d}+\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}L^{\intercal}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)L\right)^{-1}L^{\intercal}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H),

where in the last line we have applied Woodbury with
U=V^{\intercal}=\sqrt{\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)L
.
The equality is achieved because

\displaystyle(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)S^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\alpha}\underbrace{(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(H^{\dagger}H)}_{=0}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)L\left(I_{d}+\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}L^{\intercal}(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)L\right)^{-1}L^{\intercal}\underbrace{(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(H^{\dagger}H)}_{=0}

\displaystyle=0.\qed

When
\sigma>0
, we can apply Woodbury identity to sum of matrices
A
and
B
and get
(A+B)^{-1}=A^{-1}-A^{-1}B(A+B)^{-1}
. Thus,

(S+\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}H)^{-1}=S^{-1}-\alpha\sigma S^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)(S+\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}H)^{-1}

and

\displaystyle(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)(S+\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}H)^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)S^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)}_{=0}

\displaystyle\qquad-\alpha\sigma\underbrace{(I_{d}-H^{\dagger}H)S^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)}_{=0}(S+\alpha\sigma H^{\dagger}H)^{-1}(H^{\dagger}H)

\displaystyle=0.

The proof is now complete.

J.4
Lemma

10
and its proof

Lemma 10
.

With
1-\alpha_{t}\equiv\frac{\log T}{T},\penalty\ \forall t\geq 0
(which satisfies
Definition

1
), given any
p>0
,

\sum_{t=2}^{T}(1-\alpha_{t})\bar{\alpha}_{t}^{p}=\frac{1}{p}\left(1-\frac{2pc\log T}{T}\right)+\tilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Define the sum as
s_{T}
. Then,

\displaystyle s_{T}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\frac{c\log T}{T}\left(1-\frac{c\log T}{T}\right)^{pt}=\frac{c\log T}{T}\left(1-\frac{c\log T}{T}\right)^{2p}\frac{1-\left(1-\frac{c\log T}{T}\right)^{p(T-1)}}{1-\left(1-\frac{c\log T}{T}\right)^{p}}

\displaystyle=\frac{c\log T}{T}\left(1-\frac{c\log T}{T}\right)^{2p}\frac{1}{1-\left(1-\frac{c\log T}{T}\right)^{p}}(1+O(T^{-cp}))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\left(1-\frac{2pc\log T}{T}\right)+\tilde{O}(T^{-2}).\qed
